Neptuna：用于对复杂多相流进行基准测试的综合机器学习框架Neptuna: A Comprehensive Machine Learning Framework for Benchmarking Complex Multiphase Flows

哈里什·拉马钱德兰Harish Ramachandran慕尼黑工业大学 harish.ramachandran@tum.deTechnical University of Munich harish.ramachandran@tum.de

比约恩·金佩尔Björn Kimpel慕尼黑工业大学 bjoern.kimpel@tum.deTechnical University of Munich bjoern.kimpel@tum.de

托马斯·保拉Thomas Paula慕尼黑工业大学 thomas.paula@tum.deTechnical University of Munich thomas.paula@tum.de

约瑟夫·温特Josef Winter

慕尼黑工业大学 josef.winter@tum.deTechnical University of Munich josef.winter@tum.de

史蒂芬·施密特Steffen Schmidt慕尼黑工业大学 steffen.schmidt@tum.deTechnical University of Munich steffen.schmidt@tum.de

尼古拉斯·亚当斯Nikolaus Adams慕尼黑工业大学 nikolaus.adams@tum.deTechnical University of Munich nikolaus.adams@tum.de

摘要Abstract

涉及冲击和材料界面的可压缩多相流出现在气泡破裂和液滴破碎等应用中，其中强烈的非线性相互作用会产生复杂的界面变形、混合和多尺度动力学。由于同时存在可压缩性、尖锐的不连续性和多相效应，为这些流开发可靠的机器学习替代物仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们引入了第一个专门为冲击驱动的可压缩多相流设计的大型基准，包括 2.4 TB 的高保真 2D 和 3D 数据集Compressible multiphase flows involving shocks and material interfaces arise in applications such as bubble collapse and droplet breakup, where strong nonlinear interactions produce complex interface deformation, mixing, and multiscale dynamics. Developing reliable machine learning surrogates for these flows remains challenging due to the simultaneous presence of compressibility, sharp discontinuities, and multiphase effects. In this work, we introduce the first large-scale benchmark specifically designed for shock-driven compressible multiphase flows, comprising 2.4 TB of high-fidelity 2D and 3D datasets1具有冲击引起的气泡破裂和液滴破裂的特点。我们在我们的基准框架上评估不同的代理模型家族：Neptunafeaturing shock-induced bubble collapse and droplet breakup. We evaluate diverse surrogate model families on our benchmarking framework: Neptuna2，包括卷积、谱、基于变压器和预训练的 PDE 基础模型。除了标准 MSE 训练之外，我们还研究了将 MSE 与 Sobolev、接口感知和结构感知术语相结合的复合损失，以及使用 SoftAdapt 和 GradNorm 的自适应损失平衡。评估包括逐点、光谱、聚焦特征、结构和物理信息指标。结果表明，没有一个模型在所有数据集和指标上都表现最佳，而复合损失显着改善了界面保留和光谱保真度。在自适应加权策略中，与仅 MSE 训练相比，SoftAdapt 提供了最一致的改进，几乎没有任何开销。, including convolutional, spectral, transformer-based, and pre-trained PDE foundation models. Beyond standard MSE training, we investigate composite losses combining MSE with Sobolev, interface-aware, and structure-aware terms, together with adaptive loss balancing using SoftAdapt and GradNorm. Evaluation includes pointwise, spectral, featurefocused, structural, and physics-informed metrics. Results show that no single model performs best across all datasets and metrics, while composite losses significantly improve interface preservation and spectral fidelity. Among adaptive weighting strategies, SoftAdapt provides the most consistent improvements with almost no overhead compared to MSE-only training.

1 简介1 Introduction

涉及冲击波和材料界面的可压缩多相流出现在广泛的自然现象和工程应用中。冲击之间的强非线性相互作用Compressible multiphase flows involving shock waves and material interfaces arise in a wide range of natural phenomena and engineering applications. Strong nonlinear interactions between shocks

预印本。Preprint.

相边界导致复杂的动力学，例如界面变形、涡度产生、混合和拓扑变化。and phase boundaries lead to complex dynamics such as interface deformation, vorticity generation, mixing, and topological changes.

水中冲击引起的气泡破裂代表了可压缩多相流的重要状态。在气液结构中，例如与水处于机械平衡的气泡，较大的声阻抗失配会导致崩溃期间的强烈压缩和能量集中，通常会产生高压区域和射流形成。相比之下，气体-气体结构，例如空气中的重气体（例如 R22）气泡，可以隔离压缩性驱动的效应，并提供一种在不受表面张力或空化动力学影响的情况下分析冲击折射、涡度演化和混合的装置。这种现象在现实世界中有许多应用，包括用于保护水下结构的气泡幕（Würsig et al., 2000）和冲击波碎石术（Leighton, 2012）。Shock-induced air bubble collapse in water represents an important regime of compressible multiphase flows. In gas–liquid configurations, such as air bubbles in mechanical equilibrium with water, the large acoustic impedance mismatch leads to strong compression and energy focusing during collapse, often producing high-pressure regions and jet formation. In contrast, gas–gas configurations, such as heavy gas (e.g. R22) bubbles in air, isolate compressibility-driven effects and provide a setup to analyze shock refraction, vorticity evolution, and mixing without the influence of surface tension or cavitation dynamics. Such phenomena have numerous real-world applications, including bubble curtains for protecting submerged structures (Würsig et al., 2000) and shockwave lithotripsy (Leighton, 2012).

另一类重要的问题是空气中冲击引起的液滴破裂。在这种情况下，冲击和液滴之间的相互作用导致一系列由惯性力和表面张力之间的竞争控制的变形和破碎机制。根据韦伯数的不同，液滴可能会经历不同的破碎模式，例如瑞利泰勒穿孔 (RTP) 和剪切诱导夹带 (SIE)，每种破碎模式都与不同的不稳定机制相关 (Theofanous 和 Li, 2008)。这些过程与燃料雾化、喷雾动力学和高速燃烧等应用高度相关（Lefebvre 和 McDonell，2017）。Another important class of problems is shock-induced droplet breakup in air. In this case, the interaction between the shock and a liquid droplet leads to a range of deformation and breakup mechanisms governed by the competition between inertial and surface tension forces. Depending on the Weber number, the droplet may undergo different breakup modes, such as Rayleigh Taylor Piercing (RTP) and Shear Induced Entrainment (SIE), each associated with a distinct instability mechanism (Theofanous and Li, 2008). These processes are highly relevant in applications such as fuel atomization, spray dynamics, and high-speed combustion (Lefebvre and McDonell, 2017).

尽管计算流体动力学 (CFD) 求解器取得了重大进展，但由于需要高空间和时间分辨率来准确捕获流动物理场，因此模拟具有冲击的可压缩多相流的计算成本仍然很高。这些挑战导致人们对基于机器学习的替代模型越来越感兴趣，以实现更快的近似。虽然几个现有的数据集存储库捕获了可压缩流物理（Takamoto et al., 2022; Herde et al., 2024）或多相动力学（Hassan et al., 2023, 2025）的重要方面，但它们并没有在综合基准设置中共同解决可压缩性、冲击相互作用和多相效应。据我们所知，这项工作提供了第一个专门为具有冲击的可压缩多相流设计的基准数据集，从而扩展了现有公开数据集中流态的复杂性。Despite significant progress in Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) solvers, simulating compressible multiphase flows with shocks remains computationally expensive due to the need for high spatial and temporal resolution to accurately capture the flow physics. These challenges have led to increasing interest in machine learning-based surrogate models for faster approximations. While several existing dataset repositories capture important aspects of compressible flow physics (Takamoto et al., 2022; Herde et al., 2024) or multiphase dynamics (Hassan et al., 2023, 2025), they do not jointly address compressibility, shock interactions, and multiphase effects in a comprehensive benchmark setting. To the best of our knowledge, this work provides the first benchmark datasets specifically designed for compressible multiphase flows with shocks, thereby extending the complexity of flow regimes in existing publically available datasets.

我们提供一个We provide a2.4 TB 高保真 2D 和 3D 数据集集合2.4 TB collection of high-fidelity 2D and 3D datasets并研究高度非线性、多尺度和流态相关流的可靠替代模型。我们跨卷积、谱和基于注意力的架构评估 SOTA 基线，包括 ConvNeXt (and study reliable surrogate modeling for highly nonlinear, multiscale, and regime-dependent flows. We evaluate SOTA baselines across convolutional, spectral, and attention-based architectures, including ConvNeXt (下一个CNeXt）（刘等人，2022），) (Liu et al., 2022),中海油CNO（Raonic 等人，2023），(Raonic et al., 2023),FFNO（Tran 等人，2021），以及(Tran et al., 2021), and斯科特ScOT（Herde 等人，2024），并微调两个预训练模型：(Herde et al., 2024), and fine-tune two pre-trained models:波塞冬Poseidon（Herde 等人，2024）和(Herde et al., 2024) andDPOT（Hao 等人，2024）。(Hao et al., 2024).

除了评估广泛的基线之外，我们还在两种配置下训练每个模型：一种仅使用均方误差 (MSE)，另一种使用复合损失，通过旨在捕获重要流量特征的附加项来增强 MSE。具体来说，我们通过 H 包含基于梯度的惩罚Beyond evaluating a broad spectrum of baselines, we train each model under two configurations: one using only Mean Squared Error (MSE), and another using a composite loss that augments MSE with additional terms designed to capture important flow characteristics. Specifically, we include gradient-based penalties via the H1-seminorm（Hartmann 等人，2019）、以接口为中心的指标，例如以接口为中心的均方根误差（IRMSE）（Hassan 等人，2023），以及结构感知指标，如结构相似性指数（SSIM）（Nilsson 和 Akenine-Möller，2020）。这些额外的损失项是在对冲击引起的气泡破裂数据集进行广泛实验后凭经验选择的，其中它们的包含在推理过程中持续改进了所述指标或广泛的指标。为了平衡这些损失成分的贡献，我们采用了两种自适应加权策略——SoftAdapt (SA)（Heydari et al., 2019）和梯度归一化（GN）（Chen et al., 2017）——它们在训练期间定期对每个损失项及其梯度进行采样，并相应地调整各自的权重。该框架还支持自定义课程训练块，使用户能够根据时代的不同在训练的不同阶段指定不同的训练和评估策略。-seminorm (Hartmann et al., 2019), interface-focused metrics such as the Interface-focused Root Mean Square Error (IRMSE) (Hassan et al., 2023), and structure-aware measures like the Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) (Nilsson and Akenine-Möller, 2020). These additional loss terms were selected empirically after extensive experimentation on the shock-induced air bubble collapse dataset, where their inclusion consistently improved either that said metric or a broad range of metrics during inference. To balance the contributions of these loss components, we employ two adaptive weighting strategies— SoftAdapt (SA) (Heydari et al., 2019) and Gradient Normalization (GN) (Chen et al., 2017)—which periodically sample each loss term and its gradient during training and adjust their respective weights accordingly. The framework also supports custom curriculum training blocks, enabling users to specify different training and evaluation strategies at different stages of training depending on the epoch.

在推理过程中，我们报告了除用于训练的评估指标之外的一组全面的评估指标。其中包括额外的逐点测量，例如平均绝对误差 (MAE)、方差标度均方根误差 (VRMSE)（Ohana 等人，2024 年）和 HDuring inference, we report a comprehensive set of evaluation metrics beyond those used for training. These include additional pointwise measures such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Variance Scaled Root Mean Squared Error (VRMSE) (Ohana et al., 2024), and the H2-seminorm（Hartmann 等人，2019）；谱度量，如多级小波损失 (MLW)（Prantl 等人，2022）和小波频率分级 RMSE（wfRMSE）（Takamoto 等人，2022）；以及强调急剧梯度的以特征为中心的指标，例如以冲击为中心的 RMSE。我们还包括其他结构感知度量，例如皮尔逊相关系数 (PCC)（Hu 等人，2026），以及跟踪感兴趣的积分量的物理信息指标，包括气泡数据集的质量、动量、动能、液滴和熵情况下的质心位移（Takamoto 等人，2022）。-seminorm (Hartmann et al., 2019); spectral metrics like Multilevel Wavelet Loss (MLW) (Prantl et al., 2022) and wavelet frequency-binned RMSE (wfRMSE) (Takamoto et al., 2022); and feature-focused metrics that emphasize sharp gradients, such as shock-focused RMSE. We also include additional structure-aware measures like the Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) (Hu et al., 2026), as well as physics-informed metrics that track integral quantities of interest, including mass, momentum, kinetic-energy for bubble datasets, center of mass displacement in case of droplets and enstrophy (Takamoto et al., 2022) over each rollout step.

这项工作的总结贡献是：A summarized contribution of this work is:

  • A 2.4TB高保真基准数据集2.4 TB high-fidelity benchmark dataset用于冲击驱动的可压缩多相流，涵盖 2D 和 3D 中的气泡破裂和液滴破碎。for shock-driven compressible multiphase flows, covering bubble collapse and droplet breakup in 2D and 3D.

  • A 多种替代模型的系统基准systematic benchmark of diverse surrogate models，包括卷积、基于算子、基于变压器和预训练的 PDE 基础模型。, including convolutional, operatorbased, transformer-based, and pre-trained PDE foundation models.

  • 一项调查An investigation of物理和结构感知复合损耗physics- and structure-aware composite losses，将 MSE 与导数、接口和结构重点项以及自适应损失平衡相结合。, combining MSE with derivative-, interface-, and structure-focused terms and adaptive loss balancing.

  • A 以可靠性为中心的评估框架reliability-focused evaluation framework超越逐点误差扩展到光谱、结构、以流特征为中心、结构感知和物理信息的指标。extending beyond pointwise errors to spectral, structural, flow feature-focused, structure-aware and physics-informed metrics.

2 相关工作2 Related Work

开发可靠的机器学习代理不仅取决于训练数据的质量和多样性，还取决于用于优化的模型架构、训练策略和损失函数。Developing reliable machine learning surrogates depends not only on the quality and diversity of the training data, but also on the model architecture, training strategy and loss functions used for optimization.

捕捉偏微分方程动态的数据集不断扩大。早期公开的集合，例如 PDEBench（Takamoto 等人，2022）和 PDEArena（Gupta 和 Brandstetter，2022），专注于计算生成相对高效的规范偏微分方程。最近，该领域已转向日益复杂和精密的数据集，这些数据集通常需要领域专业知识来管理，如 Well（Ohana 等人，2024）、BlastNet（Chung 等人，2023）、REALM（Mao 等人，2025）、BubbleFormer（Hassan 等人，2025）和 ClimSim（Yu 等人，2025）所示。此外，RealPDEBench（Hu et al., 2026）提供了旨在微调预训练模型的实验数据集，使它们能够更好地解释在现实世界实验中观察到的效果。Datasets capturing PDE dynamics continue to expand. Early publicly available collections, such as PDEBench (Takamoto et al., 2022) and PDEArena (Gupta and Brandstetter, 2022), focused on canonical PDEs that are relatively efficient to generate computationally. More recently, the field has shifted toward increasingly complex and sophisticated datasets that often require domain expertise for their curation, as showcased by the Well (Ohana et al., 2024), BlastNet (Chung et al., 2023), REALM (Mao et al., 2025), BubbleFormer (Hassan et al., 2025), and ClimSim (Yu et al., 2025). Additionally, RealPDEBench (Hu et al., 2026) presents experimental datasets aimed at fine-tuning pre-trained models, enabling them to better account for effects observed in real-world experiments.

许多数据集论文现在随数据一起发布了自己的基准测试代码。在最近的努力中，APEBench（Koehler et al., 2024）提供了更严格的训练策略，旨在为神经算子实现稳定的长期部署。相关想法还包括用高斯噪声扰动输入以提高鲁棒性，正如 Sanchez-Gonzalez 等人所探讨的那样。 （2020）。一些著作进一步引入了课程式的训练策略，例如 Brandstetter 等人中的推进技巧。 (2022)，或在历元过程中在单步和多步自回归训练之间有效转换的训练调度程序，如 Takamoto 等人中所述。 （2023）。Many dataset papers now release their own benchmarking code alongside the data. Among recent efforts, APEBench (Koehler et al., 2024) provides more rigorous training strategies aimed at achieving stable long-horizon rollouts for neural operators. Related ideas also include perturbing inputs with Gaussian noise to improve robustness, as explored in Sanchez-Gonzalez et al. (2020). Several works have further introduced curriculum-style training strategies, such as the pushforward trick in Brandstetter et al. (2022), or training schedulers that transition efficiently between single-step and multistep autoregressive training over the course of epochs, as in Takamoto et al. (2023).

此外，人们对训练目标超出标准逐点损失的替代模型越来越感兴趣。一些研究不是仅仅依赖 MSE 或 MAE，而是优化辅助的、特定领域指标的加权组合。例如，WaveLiT（Sankaran 等人）使用 MSE 和小波域 L 的组合Furthermore, there is a growing interest in training surrogate models with objectives that go beyond standard pointwise losses. Instead of relying only on MSE or MAE, several studies optimize weighted combinations of auxiliary, domain-specific metrics. For example, WaveLiT (Sankaran et al.) uses a combination of MSE and a wavelet-domain L1损失。 PINO（Li et al., 2021）结合了 Lloss. PINO (Li et al., 2021) combines an L2带有 PDE 剩余损失的损失项。其他作品，例如 Cho 等人。 （2024b）探索了训练过程中的 Sobolev 损失，而 CRONet（Olabiyi 等人，2025）则从结构拓扑优化领域使用 SSIM 损失来指导优化。loss term with a PDE-residual loss. Other works, such as Cho et al. (2024b), explore Sobolev losses during training, while CRONet (Olabiyi et al., 2025), from the structural topology optimization domain, uses SSIM loss to guide the optimization.

3个数据集3 Datasets

在这项工作中，我们介绍了与多相流相关的六个数据集，这些数据集是通过求解可压缩欧拉方程生成的。如图 1 所示，生成的数据集涵盖了各种场景，包括二维和三维的气泡和液滴，涉及不同的材料和边界条件。为了生成这些数据集，我们采用高保真有限体积求解器 ALPACA（Hoppe 等人，2022），结合鲁棒离散方程方法（RDEMIC）（Paula 等人，2023）进行多相和界面处理。In this work, we introduce six datasets related to multiphase flows, generated by solving the compressible Euler equations. The generated datasets, as shown in Figure 1,span a wide variety of scenarios, including bubbles and droplets in both two and three dimensions, involving different materials and boundary conditions. To produce these datasets, we employ the high-fidelity finite volume solver ALPACA (Hoppe et al., 2022), incorporating the Robust Discrete Equations Method (RDEMIC) (Paula et al., 2023) for multiphase and interface treatment.

图 2 显示了用于生成表 1 中提到的维度的数据集的初始条件和边界条件。完整的模拟域由黑色长方体表示，而橙色立方体表示用于数据集的裁剪子域。我们通过考虑多个边界条件配置来丰富子域内的流动动力学。对称边界表现为无粘性墙，将入射冲击反射回子域。作为替代方案，我们通过在更大的计算域上运行模拟并随后将其裁剪到所需区域来获得不受边界效应影响的子域。我们将这些裁剪后的、无边界效应的子域称为具有开放边界。Figure 2 shows the initial and boundary conditions used to generate the datasets with the dimensions mentioned in Table 1. The complete simulation domain is depicted by the black cuboid, while the orange cube denotes the cropped subdomain used for the dataset. We enrich the flow dynamics within the subdomain by considering multiple boundary-condition configurations. Symmetry boundaries behave as inviscid walls, reflecting the incident shock back into the subdomain. As an alternative, we obtain a subdomain free from boundary effects by running the simulation on a larger computational domain and subsequently cropping it to the desired region. We refer to these cropped, boundaryeffect-free subdomains as having open boundaries.

3.1 水中冲击引起的气泡破裂（SABW）3.1 Shock-induced Air Bubble Collapse in Water (SABW)

在水中冲击引起的气泡破裂数据集中，移动的平面冲击波撞击多个气泡，导致气泡完全破裂成小碎片，这些碎片被平流向下游传播，并导致破裂的气泡发出一系列相互作用的冲击波。 A.3.1 节提供了该数据集物理原理的更详细描述。In the shock-induced air bubble collapse in water dataset, a moving planar shockwave impacts several bubbles resulting in complete breakdown of the bubbles into small fragments which get advected downstream and results in a series of interacting shockwaves emanating from the collapsed bubbles. A more detailed description of the physics of this dataset is provided in section A.3.1.

该数据集以具有不同边界条件的两种变体形式提供。在第一个变体（OOOO）中，所有边界都设置为开放，从而能够单独研究冲击-气泡相互作用和气泡间动力学。在第二个变体（SSOO）中，对称边界条件应用于南北边界，引起冲击波反射，而东部和西部边界保持开放。该设置不仅捕获了冲击和气泡之间的直接相互作用，还捕获了The dataset is provided in two variants with different boundary conditions. In the first variant (OOOO), all boundaries are set to open, enabling the study of shock–bubble interactions and inter-bubble dynamics in isolation. In the second variant (SSOO), symmetry boundary conditions are applied at the north and south boundaries, causing shockwave reflections, while the east and west boundaries remain open. This setup captures not only the direct interaction between the shock and the bubbles, as well

3.2 空气中冲击引起的液滴破碎（SDBA）3.2 Shock-induced Droplet Breakup in Air (SDBA)

该数据集捕获了外部冲击与悬浮在空气中的液滴相互作用的动态。在相互作用过程中，冲击波在液滴处发生反射、传播和衍射，从而导致表面不稳定性。根据韦伯数（代表空气动力与表面张力之比的无量纲量，主要由液滴直径和表面张力系数决定），相互作用会产生两种不同的破碎状态：瑞利-泰勒穿孔 (RTP)（图 5 和 20）（韦伯数低于 100）和剪切诱导夹带 (SIE)（图 6 和 21）（韦伯数高于 1000）。 A.3.2 节提供了这些制度的特征。由于液滴变形需要较长的模拟时间，因此实现吸收边界条件将需要过大的计算域以确保反射的冲击波不会重新进入子域。考虑到计算限制，数据集是使用对称性生成的This dataset captures the dynamics of an external shock interacting with liquid droplets suspended in air. During the interaction, the shockwave is reflected, transmitted, and diffracted at the droplets, giving rise to surface instabilities. Depending on the Weber number—a dimensionless quantity representing the ratio of aerodynamic forces to surface tension, mainly determined by droplet diameter and surface tension coefficient—the interaction results in two distinct breakup regimes: Rayleigh–Taylor Piercing (RTP) (Figures 5 and 20), for Weber number below 100 and Shear-Induced Entrainment (SIE) (Figures 6 and 21) for Weber number above 1000. The detailed flow evolution and characterization of these regimes are provided in section A.3.2. Because droplet deformation requires long simulation times, implementing absorbing boundary conditions would require an excessively large computational domain to ensure that reflected shock waves do not re-enter the subdomain. Keeping computational constraints in mind, the dataset is generated using symmetry

3.3 空气中冲击引起的 R22 气泡破裂 (SRBA)3.3 Shock-induced R22 Bubble Collaspe in Air (SRBA)

与 SABW 和 SDBA 数据集相比，空气中 R22 气泡的冲击相互作用代表可压缩的气体-气体配置。该设置隔离了密度驱动的界面动力学，并能够研究冲击折射、斜斜涡度生成和 Richtmyer-Meshkov 型不稳定性，而不会受到液滴破碎数据集中观察到的表面张力的混杂影响。与气泡破裂不同，由于 R22 气泡密度较高，观察到蘑菇形变形，如图 7 所示。对于该数据集，所有边界均设置为 Open BC，图 24 中描述了一组完整的场变量，A.3.3 节中描述了流动物理场。In contrast to the SABW and SDBA datasets, the shock-interaction with R22 bubbles in air represents a compressible gas-gas configuration. This setup isolates density-driven interface dynamics and enables the study of shock refraction, baroclinic vorticity generation, and Richtmyer–Meshkov-type instabilities without the confounding effects of surface tension as observed in the droplet breakup datasets. Unlike the air bubble collapse, a mushroom shaped deformation is observed as seen in Figure 7 owing to the higher density of the R22 bubble. For this dataset, all boundaries are set to Open BC, a complete set of field variables is depicted in Figure 24 and the flow physics is described in section A.3.3.

4 基准测试4 Benchmarking

在这项工作中，我们提供了一组经过训练的二维和三维基线模型，包括随机初始化的网络和根据预训练权重进行微调的模型。对于 2D 设置，随机初始化的基线由 ConvNeXt、CNO、50M 参数变体组成，In this work, we provide a collection of trained two- and three-dimensional baseline models, including both randomly initialized networks and models fine-tuned from pre-trained weights. For the 2D setting, the randomly initialized baselines consist of 50M-parameter variants of ConvNeXt, CNO,

6

FFNO和ScOT，而预训练的基线包括Poseidon-B（122M参数）和DPOTM（150M参数）。对于 3D 设置，我们对 FFNO 和 ConvNeXt 的 10M 参数变体进行基准测试。 2D 和 3D 基线均在固定的 24 小时计算预算下进行训练。 B.1 节提供了详细的模型特定超参数，表 11 总结了总体训练超参数。FFNO, and ScOT, while the pre-trained baselines include Poseidon-B (122M parameters) and DPOTM (150M parameters). For the 3D setting, we benchmark 10M-parameter variants of FFNO and ConvNeXt. Both the 2D and 3D baselines are trained under a fixed 24-hour computational budget. Detailed model-specific hyperparameters are provided in Section B.1, while the overall training hyperparameters are summarized in Table 11.

对于从随机权重初始化的模型，学习问题被定义为从数据集轨迹中提取四个时间快照作为输入并预测后续状态。我们进一步根据与数据集相关的模拟参数来调节模型，如图 1 所示，使用这些参数来调制基线架构的归一化层（Perez 等人，2017）。培训和评估协议详见 B.2 和 B.3 节。为了微调预训练的 Poseidon-B 模型，我们保留原始的 1:1 输入输出快照设置 (Herde et al., 2024)，而为了微调 DPOT-M，我们遵循原始的 10:1 输入输出配置 (Hao et al., 2024)。For models initialized from random weights, the learning problem is defined to extract four temporal snapshots from a dataset trajectory as input and predict the subsequent state. We further condition the model on the simulation parameters associated with the dataset, summarized in Figure 1, by using these parameters to modulate the normalization layers of the baseline architectures (Perez et al., 2017). The training and evaluation protocols are detailed in sections B.2 and B.3. For finetuning the pre-trained Poseidon-B model, we retain the original 1-to-1 input–output snapshot setting (Herde et al., 2024), while for finetuning DPOT-M, we follow the original 10-to-1 input–output configuration (Hao et al., 2024).

5 结果与结论5 Results and Conclusion

在本节中，我们将展示并比较在第 3 节中介绍的六个数据集上使用三种训练损失策略获得的基准结果。第 4 节中描述的所有基线模型均以 256 的分辨率进行训练In this section, we present and compare benchmark results obtained using three training-loss strategies across the six datasets introduced in section 3. All baseline models described in section 4 are trained at a resolution of 2562对于 2D 数据集和 128for the 2D datasets and 1283对于 3D 数据集。在我们的消融中，我们考虑三种损失配置：（i）仅使用均方误差（MSE）进行训练； (ii) 使用复合目标进行训练，其组成权重使用 SA 进行调整； (iii) 使用相同的复合目标进行训练，但使用 GN 进行自适应损失加权。复合目标将 MSE 与旨在捕获流的互补方面的附加项相结合：Hfor the 3D datasets. In our ablations, we consider three loss configurations: (i) training with mean squared error (MSE) alone; (ii) training with a composite objective whose component weights are adapted using SA; and (iii) training with the same composite objective, but with adaptive loss-weighting performed using GN. The composite objective combines MSE with additional terms designed to capture complementary aspects of the flow: the H1-seminorm (H1) 惩罚空间梯度中的误差，结构相似性指数 (SSIM) 鼓励保留局部窗口内的结构，而界面聚焦 RMSE (IRMSE) 则强调气泡和液滴相边界附近的误差。 SA 和 GN 都会在训练期间动态调整这些损失分量的相对权重。从两个角度分析这些训练策略的额外计算开销：峰值 GPU 内存需求和一个 epoch 所需的时间。 C.1 节报告了调查结果。-seminorm (H1) penalizes errors in spatial gradients, the Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) encourages preservation of structure inside local windows and the interface-focused RMSE (IRMSE) emphasizes errors near bubble and droplet phase boundaries. Both SA and GN dynamically adjust the relative weights of these loss components during training. The additional computational overhead of these training strategies are analyzed from two perspectives: peak GPU memory requirement and time required for one epoch. The findings are reported in section C.1.

我们使用更广泛的 12 个评估指标进行推理，如表 13 中所述。图 8、9 和 10 总结了测试轨迹和三种训练损失策略的推出步骤的平均代表性结果。对于每个数据集，我们报告十二个指标中的五个，即。 VRMSE、MLW、IRMSE、SSIM 和 RMSE 关于兴趣-熵 (iqRMSE-熵) 的域积分量。选择这些指标来涵盖本工作中考虑的五个评估类别：逐点指标、光谱指标、聚焦特征指标、结构感知指标和物理信息指标。为了能够比较不同尺度的指标，首先对给定数据集的所有基线模型和训练损失策略对原始指标值进行最小-最大归一化。然后通过从 1 中减去标准化值来转换标准化值，从而反转比例，以便较低的原始误差对应于雷达图中较高的分数。 B.4 节提供了有关指标的更多详细信息，B.5 节描述了损失加权策略。 C.2 节介绍了自回归推出基线时误差的演变。 C.3 节介绍了所有数据集和基线上跨轨迹和部署聚合的完整总体指标集。 C.4 节显示了在所呈现的数据集上进行自回归期间的选择性基线预测。We perform inference using a broader set of 12 evaluation metrics, as described in Table 13. Figures 8, 9, and 10 summarize representative results averaged across the test trajectories and the rollout steps for the three training-loss strategies. For each dataset, we report five of the twelve metrics, viz. VRMSE, MLW, IRMSE, SSIM and RMSE on the domain-integrated quantity of interest- enstrophy (iqRMSE-enstrophy). These metrics are selected to cover the five evaluation categories considered in this work: pointwise, spectral, feature-focused, structure-aware, and physics-informed metrics. To enable comparison across metrics with different scales, the raw metric values are first min-max normalized across all baseline models and training-loss strategies for a given dataset. The normalized values are then transformed by subtracting them from one, thereby reversing the scale so that lower original errors correspond to higher scores in the radar charts. Further details on the metrics are provided in section B.4, while the loss-weighting strategies are described in section B.5. The evolution of the errors as the baselines are autoregressively rolled out are presented in section C.2. The complete set of overall metrics aggregated across trajectories and rollouts, on all datasets and baselines are presented in section C.3. Selective baseline predictions during autoregressive rollout on the presented datasets are shown in section C.4.

5.1 训练损失策略比较5.1 Comparison of training loss strategies

在本节中，我们比较上述三种不同训练损失策略的结果。在所有 2D 基线中，经过微调的 DPOT-M 在所提供的指标上始终优于其他基线。这可能归因于丰富的预训练权重或 10 个快照的历史输入序列。我们在本节的讨论中省略了经过微调的 Poseidon-B 基线，因为尽管它具有最大的参数数量，但它在几乎所有指标上的表现都比其他基线差得多。这可能是由于模型没有通过调节参数或通过包含有助于区分不同模拟轨迹的历史快照来合并调节的机制。In this section we compare the results from the above-mentioned three different training loss strategies. Among all the 2D baselines, fine-tuned DPOT-M consistently outperforms the other baselines on the presented metrics. This could be attributed to either the rich pre-trained weights or the historic input sequence of 10 snapshots. We omit the fine-tuned Poseidon-B baseline from our discussion in this section because it performs substantially worse than the other baselines across nearly all metrics, despite having the largest parameter count. This could be attributed to the model not having a mechanism to incorporate conditioning, either through conditioning parameters or through inclusion of historic snapshots which help in distinguishing the different simulation trajectories.

从图 8、9 和 10 中可以进一步观察到，FFNO 在 2D 气泡破裂数据集上始终优于 CNeXt。这表明谱算子模型可能更适合A further observation from Figures 8, 9, and 10 is that FFNO consistently outperforms CNeXt on the 2D bubble-collapse datasets. This suggests that spectral operator models may be better suited to

7

在这些情况下，因为气泡在最初的几个时间步内崩溃，并且动力学不再由明确定义的界面主导。因此，CNeXt 作为卷积边缘检测器的优势变得不那么明显。相比之下，对于冲击液滴破碎数据集，液滴在变形和破碎时保留了相干界面，使动力学更加局部化并且结构保持不变。这可以解释为什么 CNeXt 在这些情况下表现略好于 FFNO。在两种复合损失策略之间，与 GN 相比，SA 在基线和数据集上提供了更一致的改进。these cases, since the bubbles collapse within the first few timesteps and the dynamics are no longer dominated by a well-defined interface. As a result, the advantages of CNeXt as a convolutional edge detector become less pronounced. In contrast, for the shock-droplet breakup datasets, the droplets retain coherent interfaces as they deform and fragment, making the dynamics more localized and structure-preserving. This may explain why CNeXt performs slightly better than FFNO in these cases. Between the two composite-loss strategies, SA provides a more consistent improvement across the baselines and datasets compared to GN.

图 10：雷达图显示了在六个数据集上使用复合损失和 GN 损失加权训练的基线，并根据五个推理指标（VRMSE、MLW、IRMSE、SSIM 和 iqRMSEenstropy）进行评估。Figure 10: Radar plot showcasing baselines trained with composite loss and GN loss-weighting across six datasets, evaluated on five inference metrics—VRMSE, MLW, IRMSE, SSIM, and iqRMSEenstrophy.

现在，我们通过分析从 2D 基线的每个评估类别中选择的代表性指标来更详细地研究这些趋势。单独使用 MSE 训练的模型在逐点度量 VRMSE 上的表现与使用两种复合损失策略训练的模型相当（公式 28）。如表 22、27、32 和 37 所示，大多数基线的 VRMSE 低于 1。这表明模型能够相当好地捕获平均背景流，并且复合目标并未为该逐点指标提供实质性优势。We now examine these trends in more detail by analyzing the representative metrics selected from each evaluation category for the 2D baselines. The models trained with MSE alone perform comparably to those trained with the two composite-loss strategies on the pointwise metric VRMSE (Equation 28). As shown in Tables 22, 27, 32, and 37, most baselines achieve VRMSE below 1. This indicates that the models are able to capture the mean background flow reasonably well, and that the composite objectives do not provide a substantial advantage for this pointwise metric.

对于以界面为中心的 RMSE，复合训练的效果更为明显。对于 bubblecollapse 数据集，基于 GN 的复合训练将基线的 IRMSE 平均提高了约 5-7%（参见表 24、29、34）。相比之下，对于冲击-液滴相互作用数据集，改进要大得多，当使用 SA 和 GN 的复合损失时达到约 40-50%（参见表 39）。这种差异可以通过两个问题类中接口演变的性质来解释。气泡破裂后，通过密度阈值获得的界面变得难以识别，因为气泡破碎成非常小的The effect of composite training is more pronounced for the interface-focused RMSE. For the bubblecollapse datasets, GN-based composite training improves IRMSE by approximately 5-7% on average across the baselines (Refer Tables 24, 29, 34). In contrast, for the shock-droplet interaction dataset, the improvement is substantially larger, reaching approximately 40-50% when using composite losses with SA and GN (Refer Table 39). This difference can be explained by the nature of the interface evolution in the two problem classes. After the bubble collapse, the interface, obtained by thresholding the density, becomes difficult to identify because the bubbles fragment into very small

8

结构。然而，在冲击液滴的情况下，液滴界面在变形和破碎过程中保持更加连贯，使其更容易检测，因此对界面感知训练更敏感。structures. In the shock-droplet case, however, the droplet interface remains more coherent during deformation and breakup, making it easier to detect and therefore more responsive to interface-aware training.

当使用复合损失进行训练时，所有基线的光谱度量 MLW 的所有基线均观察到显着的改进。当使用复合损失进行训练时，小波频率分档 RMSE 的高频分量也有小但一致的改进，如表 23、28、33、38 所示。这些改进表明，我们的复合目标中提出的指标有助于减少通常由纯点目标引入的低频偏差，从而更好地恢复精细尺度结构。Significant improvements were observed for all baselines for the spectral metric-MLW across all baselines when trained with composite loss. There is also a small but consistent improvement in the high-frequency component of the wavelet frequency-binned RMSE when trained with composite loss as seen in Tables 23, 28, 33, 38. These improvements suggest that the metrics presented in our composite objective help reduce the low-frequency bias typically introduced by purely pointwise objectives, leading to better recovery of fine-scale structures.

在所有数据集和基线中，结构感知 SSIM 指标也得到了一致的改进，特别是在使用 SA，然后使用 GN 时，如表 25、30、35、40、45 和 50 所示。这种趋势可能部分归因于将 SSIM 作为复合训练目标中的术语之一。然而，我们强调，将指标纳入训练目标并不一定能保证在推理时提高该指标的性能；相反，观察到的 SSIM 改进是特定于此处考虑的设置的经验结果。推理过程中 SSIM 指标的改进也与更好的相干结构预测相关，如图 36 所示。A consistent improvement is also observed for the structure-aware SSIM metric across all datasets and baselines, especially when using SA, followed by GN as shown in Tables 25, 30, 35, 40, 45 and 50. This trend may be partly attributed to the inclusion of SSIM as one of the terms in the composite training objective. However, we emphasize that incorporating a metric into the training objective does not necessarily guarantee improved performance on that metric at inference time; rather, the observed improvement in SSIM is an empirical outcome specific to the settings considered here. The improvement in SSIM metric during inference is also associated with better coherent structure prediction, as illustrated in Figure 36.

流场的熵计算为绝对逐点涡量的积分和（参见表 19）。对于域集成熵度量（iqRMSE-enstropy），我们观察到在训练时使用复合损失没有显着优势。在某种程度上，这对于表 26、31、36、41、46 和 51 中提出的其他基于物理的指标来说也是如此。一个例外是 ScOT 基线，它从选定数据集的复合训练中受益匪浅，特别是 2D R22 气泡崩溃和 2D 冲击-液滴相互作用案例。Enstrophy of a flow field is computed as the integral summation of the absolute pointwise vorticities (Refer Table 19). For the domain integrated enstrophy metric (iqRMSE-enstrophy), we observe that there is no significant advantage of using composite losses while training. To a certain extent this holds true for other physics-based metrics presented in Tables 26, 31, 36, 41, 46 and 51. One exception is the ScOT baseline, which benefits substantially from composite training on selected datasets, particularly the 2D R22 bubble-collapse and 2D shock-droplet interaction cases.

3D 基线的性能通常比 2D 基线差。如图 8、9 和 10 中的雷达图所示，在报告的指标中，FFNO 的表现优于 ConvNeXt。此外，FFNO 始终受益于复合损失训练，显示出所有选定指标的改进。然而，应该谨慎解释这些趋势。 3D 实验受到 24 小时训练限制的限制，导致训练次数比 2D 设置要少得多。在对 3D 环境中复合损失的相对优势得出明确结论之前，需要进行更广泛的培训。The 3D baselines generally perform worse than their 2D counterparts. As shown in the radar charts in Figures 8, 9, and 10, FFNO outperforms ConvNeXt across the reported metrics. Moreover, FFNO benefits consistently from composite-loss training, showing improvements across all selected metrics. However, these trends should be interpreted with caution. The 3D experiments were constrained by a 24-hour training limit, resulting in substantially fewer training epochs than in the 2D setting. More extensive training is required before drawing firm conclusions about the relative benefits of the composite losses in the 3D setting.

5.2 结论5.2 Conclusion

在这项工作中，我们从可压缩多相流领域引入了六个新数据集。我们为 2D 数据集提供了六个基线和为 3D 数据集提供了两个基线。每个基线都使用三种不同的损失策略进行训练：单独的 MSE，以及使用 SoftAdapt 和 GradNorm 的自适应损失加权的复合损失。从结果中，我们可以得出结论，没有一个基线在所有数据集和指标中都表现最佳，并且该替代指标的选择显然取决于感兴趣的指标。然而，结果确实表明，使用具有自适应损失加权策略的复合损失可以导致一系列指标的实质性改进，特别是那些关注界面质量和频谱内容的指标。在这两种自适应损失权重策略中，与 GradNorm 相比，SoftAdapt 在基线和数据集上提供了更一致的改进，同时计算效率也更高。总之，我们的工作强调了将此类复杂的流数据集和捕获数据不同方面的多样化评估指标纳入 SciML 社区的重要性，以促进稳健的数据驱动代理模型的开发取得进一步进展。In this work, we introduce six new datasets from the regime of compressible multiphase flows. We provide benchmarks on six baselines for the 2D datasets and two baselines for the 3D datasets. Each baseline is trained with three different loss strategies: MSE alone, and composite losses with adaptive loss-weighting using SoftAdapt and GradNorm. From the results, one can conclude that there is not a single baseline which performs best across all datasets and metrics and the choice of this surrogate would clearly depend on the metric of interest. However, the results do indicate that using composite losses with adaptive loss-weighting strategies can lead to substantial improvements across a range of metrics, especially those focused on interface quality and spectral content. Among the two adaptive loss-weighting strategies, SoftAdapt provides a more consistent improvement across the baselines and datasets compared to GradNorm, while also being more computationally efficient. In conclusion, our work highlights the importance of incorporating such complex flow datasets and diverse evaluation metrics that capture different aspects of the data into the SciML community to foster further advances in the development of robust data-driven surrogate models.

局限性和未来的工作Limitations and Future Work：替代模型在复杂流动问题中捕获精细尺度细节的能力仍然存在显着差距。例如，尽管这些次级液滴是 SIE 体系的一个定义特征，但所评估的替代模型都无法准确地恢复从初级液滴表面分离的小液滴。因此，未来的工作可以集中于改进小规模界面动力学和破碎过程的处理。通过更具表现力的调节策略也可以实现进一步的改进。特别是，在训练期间合并有关边界条件的信息可以提高替代模型在不同模拟设置中的通用性。这项工作中为复合损失选择的训练指标是在分析大量实验的结果并有选择地选择要包含在复合训练中的指标后获得的: There remains a significant gap in the ability of surrogate models to capture fine-scale details in complex flow problems. For example, none of the evaluated surrogate models were able to accurately recover the small droplets that detach from the surface of the primary droplet, although these secondary droplets are a defining characteristic of the SIE regime. Future work could therefore focus on improving the treatment of small-scale interface dynamics and fragmentation processes. Further improvements may also be achieved through more expressive conditioning strategies. In particular, incorporating information about boundary conditions during training could improve the generalizability of surrogate models across different simulation setups. The training metrics selected in this work for the composite losses were obtained after analyzing results from a broad list of experiments and selectively picking the metric to be included in the composite train

9

仅当在评估过程中在所述指标或一组不同的指标上获得正相关时才会出现损失。此外，对替代预测进行更详细的现场分析将有助于识别特定的故障模式并指导改进架构和培训策略的开发。最后，基准可以扩展到包括更具挑战性的数据集，例如反应多相流和具有相变的多相流，以进一步突破该领域替代建模的极限。loss only when a positive correlation is obtained on the said metric or a diverse set of metrics during evaluation. In addition, a more detailed field-wise analysis of surrogate predictions would help identify specific failure modes and guide the development of improved architectures and training strategies. Finally, the benchmark could be extended to include more challenging datasets, such as reacting multiphase flows and multiphase flows with phase change, to further push the limits of surrogate modeling in this domain.

数据集详细信息A Dataset Details

这些数据集在 HuggingFace 上公开提供：https://huggingface.co/FluidVerse。The datasets are made publicly available on HuggingFace at https://huggingface.co/FluidVerse.

A.1 数据集组织A.1 Dataset Organization

每个数据集分为训练集和测试集，并使用分层组织以 HDF5 格式存储。在顶层，定义了组，其名称编码了指定每个轨迹的初始条件的参数。在每个组内，时间序列字段数据存储在形状中Each dataset is divided into training and testing sets and stored in HDF5 format using a hierarchical organization. At the top level, groups are defined, with their names encoding the parameters that specify the initial conditions of each trajectory. Within each group, the time-series field data is stored in the shapeT × C × Xres × Yres × Zres, 其中, whereT、C、Xres、Yres、ZresT, C, Xres, Yres, Zres分别表示 X、Y 和 Z 方向的时间步数、通道数和分辨率。下面提供了这种层次结构的说明：denote the number of timesteps, channels, and resolution in the X, Y and Z direction respectively. An illustration of this hierarchical structure is provided below:

组：005_Mas1.30_sb1_Ax0.0078_Ay0.0179_Az0.0145_Ar0.0022Group: 005_Mas1.30_sb1_Ax0.0078_Ay0.0179_Az0.0145_Ar0.0022

领域：密度Field: density
形状：（51，Shape: (51,		 1, 128, 128, 128) 数据类型：float64Dtype: float64
字段：密度_1Field: density_1

在数据加载期间，从组名称中提取模拟参数，使用最小最大归一化进行缩放，并将其作为条件参数传递到基线架构的归一化层，如 B.2 节中所述。During dataloading, the simulation parameters from the group name are extracted, scaled using minmax normalization and passed to the normalization layer of the baseline architecture as conditioning parameters as described in section B.2.

A.2 数据集规范A.2 Dataset Specifications

正如正文中提到的，数据集是使用高保真有限体积求解器 ALPCA 生成的。在此框架内，采用界面捕获的鲁棒离散方程方法（RDIMIC）通过求解二维和三维可压缩欧拉方程来生成地面实况轨迹。矢量表示法的控制方程如下：As mentioned in the main text, the datasets are generated using the high-fidelity finite volume solver ALPACA. Within this framework, the Robust Discrete Equations Method for Interface Capturing (RDEMIC) is employed to produce ground-truth trajectories by solving the two- and threedimensional compressible Euler equations. The governing equations in vector notation are given as follows:

为了闭合上述控制方程，需要一个附加的状态方程 (EOS)，它将压力、密度和内能联系起来。在这项工作中，采用硬化气体 EOS 来生成所有数据集，并由下式给出To close the above governing equations, an additional Equation of State (EOS) is required which relates pressure, density, and internal energy. In this work, the stiffened-gas EOS is employed for the generation of all datasets, and is given by

哪里wherep表示流体压力，denotes the fluid pressure,ρ质量密度，the mass density,e比内能，以及the specific internal energy, andγ模型常数。参数a model constant. The parameterp刚性代表流体预压缩的贡献。stiff represents the contribution from fluid pre-compression.

模拟使用 HLLC Riemann 求解器（Toro，2013）。如果水中气泡破裂，则使用 HLLC-LM 求解器，它可以纠正与冲击波横向的低马赫数流相关的冲击不稳定性（Fleischmann 等人，2020）。采用 WENO5 空间重建（Jiang 和 Shu，1996）来实现高精度和稳健的冲击捕获。使用三阶龙格-库塔方案（Gottlieb和Shu，1998）进行时间积分，确保流动物理的稳定和准确的演化。The simulations use the HLLC Riemann solver (Toro, 2013). In case of air-bubble collapse in water, the HLLC-LM solver is used, which remedies shock instabilities associated with the lowMach-number flow in transverse direction to the shock wave (Fleischmann et al., 2020). A WENO5 spatial reconstruction (Jiang and Shu, 1996) is employed to achieve high accuracy and robust shock capturing. Time integration is carried out using a third-order Runge-Kutta scheme (Gottlieb and Shu, 1998), ensuring stable and accurate evolution of the flow physics.

A.3 底座设置A.3 Base Setup

数据集捕获了气泡和液滴在外部冲击波作用下随时间演变的行为。计算域分为震前区域和震后区域。一旦冲击波与气泡或液滴相互作用，产生的冲击后流动在很大程度上决定了它们的变形和随后的动力学。激波马赫数（The datasets capture the time-evolving behavior of bubbles and droplets subjected to an external shock wave. The computational domain is partitioned into pre-shock and post-shock regions. Once the shock wave interacts with the bubble or droplet, the resulting post-shock flow largely governs their deformation and subsequent dynamics. The shock Mach number (Ms）作为表征该震后状态的一个有影响力的无量纲参数。对于每个初始条件，) serves as an influential non-dimensional parameter characterizing this post-shock regime. For each initial condition, the

16

震后密度、速度和压力被设置为域左边界的狄利克雷边界条件。post-shock density, velocity and pressure are set as Dirichlet boundary conditions in the left boundary of the domain.

A.3.1 水中冲击引起的气泡破裂（SABW）A.3.1 Shock-induced Air Bubble Collapse in Water (SABW)

在多相流中，关键的重要领域之一是密度的演化。在这里，我们研究了当外部冲击波与水中的圆柱形气泡相互作用时其演变，如图 11 所示。In a multiphase flow, one of the key important fields is the evolution of the density. Here, we examine its evolution when an external shock wave interacts with a cylindrical air bubble in water, as shown in Figure 11. Att = 1 . 5 µs，冲击波到达气泡，导致内部空气因传递的冲击而加速。随后，水射流开始从上游侧穿透气泡，如在, the shock wave reaches the bubble, causing the air inside to accelerate due to the transmitted shock. Subsequently, a water jet begins to penetrate the bubble from the upstream side, as observed att = 3 . 9 µs。由. Byt = 4 . 8 µs，该射流冲击气泡的下游界面，在该域中产生局部峰值压力，通常称为水锤效应。这种现象产生径向传播的冲击波，并导致气泡在二维圆柱形情况下破碎成两个碎片，或在三维球形情况下破碎成环形结构。由此产生的碎片在径向冲击的影响下进一步破碎，产生额外的压力波，如图所示, this jet impacts the downstream interface of the bubble, generating a localized peak pressure in the domain, commonly referred to as the water hammer effect. This phenomenon produces a radially propagating shock wave and leads to the breakup of the bubble into two fragments in the two-dimensional cylindrical case, or into a toroidal structure in the three-dimensional spherical case. The resulting fragments undergo further breakup under the influence of the radial shock, producing additional pressure waves, as seen att = 8 . 4 µs。最终，破碎的气泡随水流向下游平流输送。. Eventually, the fragmented bubble is advected downstream with the flow.

图 11：2D-SBAW-OOOO 数据集密度场的时间演化。Figure 11: Time evolution of density field for the 2D-SBAW-OOOO dataset.

SABW 配置的冲击后属性是使用正常冲击关系确定的（Le Métayer 和 Saurel，2016），如方程 3 所示。如基础设置中所述，该域分为冲击前区域和冲击后区域；对于 SABW，域中的流体是水，因此水的属性用于计算预激波值。在这个配方中，The post-shock properties for the SABW configuration are determined using the normal shock relations (Le Métayer and Saurel, 2016), as given in Equation 3. As discussed in the base setup, the domain is divided into pre- and post-shock regions; in the case of SABW, the fluid in the domain is water, and thus the properties of water are used to compute the pre-shock values. The comparison shows good agreement with the reference, with the solver accurately capturing the key events over time. The slight discrepancy observed in the maximum peak pressure throughout the simulation is attributed to differences in interface treatment: the reference employs a level-set method, whereas the present solver uses a diffuse interface approach, which leads to some smearing of peak values.

A.3.2 空气中冲击引起的液滴破碎（SDBA）A.3.2 Shock-induced Droplet Breakup in Air (SDBA)

冲击引起的液滴破碎数据集构成了两种不同的破碎模式，即瑞利泰勒穿孔（RTP）和剪切诱导夹带（SIE）。这些模式之间的转变主要由韦伯数 (We) 决定，它表示空气动力与表面张力的比率。The shock induced droplet breakup datasets constitute two distinct breakup modes, namely Rayleigh Taylor Piercing (RTP) and Shear Induced Entrainmet (SIE). The transition between these modes is primarily determined by the Weber number (We), which represents the ratio of aerodynamic forces to surface tension.

在上式中，In the above equation,D是液滴直径，is the droplet diameter andσ是表面张力系数。is the surface tension coefficient.

当冲击波撞击液滴时，初始响应（很大程度上与韦伯数无关）是液滴变平的变形阶段。这种变形是由其表面周围的不均匀压力分布引起的。接下来的阶段是实际的破裂，这在很大程度上受韦伯数的控制。 RTP 分解状态通常发生在韦伯数大约 10 到 100 之间，而 SIE 状态在更高的值（We > 1000）中占主导地位，中间的韦伯数代表两种模式之间的过渡（Theofanous 和 Li，2008）。When a shock wave impacts a droplet, the initial response—largely independent of the Weber number—is a deformation phase in which the droplet flattens. This deformation arises from a nonuniform pressure distribution around its surface. The subsequent stage is the actual breakup, which is strongly governed by the Weber number. The RTP breakup regime typically occurs for Weber numbers between roughly 10 and 100, whereas the SIE regime dominates at much higher values (We > 1000), with intermediate Weber numbers representing a transition between the two modes (Theofanous and Li, 2008).

在 SIE 体系中，破碎主要是由沿液滴表面作用的强剪切力驱动的。液滴变平后，赤道附近会出现剪切引起的扰动；这些不稳定性起源于液滴在第一阶段变平后的液滴赤道附近，并沿着液滴表面平流传播。随着液滴与周围气体之间的相对速度增加，这些扰动由于开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性而增加，最终从液滴中剥离液体并在下游产生细小的液滴。相反，在 RTP 状态下，相对较强的表面张力抑制了这种剪切不稳定性的增长，从而保持了更光滑的界面。随着变形的进行，随着周围的气体渗透并刺穿液体，液滴的上游侧变得凹入。与 RTP 不同，SIE 体系的特点是质量持续且逐渐损失，通常会导致下游产生雾状液滴（Winter 等人，2019）。In the SIE regime, breakup is driven mainly by strong shear forces acting along the droplet surface. After the droplet has flattened, shear-induced disturbances emerge near the equator; these instabilities originate near the droplet equator after the droplet has flattened out in the first phase and are advected along the droplet surface. As the relative velocity between the droplet and the surrounding gas increases, these disturbances grow due to Kelvin–Helmholtz instability, eventually stripping liquid from the droplet and producing fine droplets downstream. In contrast, in the RTP regime, relatively stronger surface tension suppresses the growth of such shear instabilities, maintaining a smoother interface. As deformation progresses, the upstream side of the droplet becomes concave as the surrounding gas penetrates and pierces the liquid. Unlike RTP, the SIE regime is characterized by a continuous and gradual loss of mass, often resulting in a mist of droplets downstream (Winter et al., 2019).

为了确定 SDBA 设置的冲击后特性，我们使用方程 3 所示的冲击关系来计算冲击后的流动特性（Anderson，1990）。For determining the post-shock properties for the SDBA setup, we use the shock relations as shown in Equation 3 to compute the flow properties behind the shock (Anderson, 1990).

A.3.3 空气中冲击引起的 R22 气泡破裂 (SRBA)A.3.3 Shock-induced R22 Bubble Breakup in Air (SRBA)

SRBA 情况的动态与 SABW 情况明显不同。由于 R22 气泡比周围空气密度更大，因此气泡内传输的冲击波的传播速度比入射冲击波慢，如图 23(a) 所示。当传递的冲击波向气泡下游侧汇聚时，会形成一个小尖点，如图 23(b) 所示，最终演变成薄 R22 射流，如图 23(c) 所示。在同一时间间隔内，小涡流沿着界面形成，并随后在它们向气泡下游移动时被放大。The dynamics for the SRBA case is notably different from the SABW scenario. Since the R22 bubble is denser than the surrounding air, the transmitted shock within the bubble propagates more slowly than the incident shock, as illustrated in Figure 23(a). As the transmitted shock converges toward the downstream side of the bubble, a small cusp forms, as seen in Figure 23(b), which eventually evolves into a thin R22 jet, shown in Figure 23(c). During the same time interval, small vortices develop along the interface and are subsequently amplified as they move downstream of the bubble.

A.4 数据集下采样A.4 Downsampling of Datasets

粗网格上的直接模拟通常价值有限，因为边界层或界面变形等关键现象很难解决，而在高分辨率数据上训练替代模型在计算上仍然具有挑战性。一种常见的解决方法是生成高分辨率的数据集，然后通过对原始变量（例如相邻单元的速度或压力）进行平均，将其下采样到较粗糙的网格。然而，这种简单的平均并不适合可压缩流，因为它不能保留关键的守恒量，例如动量和能量。在本节中，我们提出了一种保守的下采样策略，可确保在对数据集进行下采样时保持总质量、动量和能量。Direct simulations on coarse grids are often of limited value because key phenomena, such as boundary layers or interface deformation, are poorly resolved, while training surrogate models on high-resolution data remains computationally challenging. A common workaround is to generate datasets at high resolution and then downsample them to a coarser grid by averaging primitive variables, such as velocity or pressure over neighboring cells. However, this naive averaging is not appropriate for compressible flows, since it does not preserve key conserved quantities such as momentum and energy. In this section, we present a conservative downsampling strategy that ensures conservation of total mass, momentum and energy when downsampling the dataset.

A.4.1 方法论A.4.1 Methodology

当使用 ALPAC 生成更高分辨率的数据集时，我们还存储每个单独相的密度。我们的方法被设计为通用的并且适用于任何多相求解器，原则上应该提供对每个相的密度的访问。由于这项工作中的所有数据集都涉及两个相，因此除了混合密度场之外，我们还保存相密度。以下段落描述了这些相密度在下采样过程中的使用。When generating higher-resolution datasets with ALPACA, we also store the densities of each individual phase. Our methodology is designed to be general and applicable to any multiphase solver, which should, in principle, provide access to the densities of each phase. As all datasets in this work involve two phases, we save the phasic densities in addition to the mixed density field. The use of these phasic densities in the downsampling process is described in the following paragraphs.

多相模拟的另一个关键属性是体积分数，Another key property of multiphase simulation is the volume fraction,α，定义给定单元中每个相的份额。在这里，, defining the share of each phase in a given cell. Here,α1 定义了第一相的体积分数，1 defines the volume fraction of phase-1 andα第 2 阶段的 2 可以计算为2 for phase-2 can be computed asα 2 = 1 - α− α1. 在仅存在一相的细胞中，1. 对域进行下采样时，需要满足这三个方程。质量守恒规定域中的总质量在下采样期间必须保持守恒。因此，细网格单元中的总质量必须等于粗网格单元的总质量，从而得出第 1 相 (Φ1) 的方程 6，类似的方程也适用于第 2 相 (Φ2)。When downsampling the domain, these three equations need to be satisfied. The mass conservation states that the total mass in the domain must remain conserved during downsampling. Hence, the total mass in the fine grid cells must equal the total mass of the coarse grid cell, leading to Equation 6 for phase-1 (Φ1), a similar equation is applicable for phase-2 (Φ2) as well.

我们从二维冲击引起的空气中液滴破碎 (SDBA) 数据集中选择随机轨迹，并比较两种下采样方法获得的结果。平均方法通过对相邻单元进行平均来独立地对每个原始场变量（例如密度、速度）进行下采样。平均场的质量、动量和能量在下采样后计算，如图 25 所示。相比之下，保守方法对守恒量进行运算，例如动量 (We choose a random trajectory from the 2D-shock-induced droplet breakup in air (SDBA) dataset and compare the results obtained from both downsampling methods. The average method downsamples each primitive field variable independently (e.g. density, velocity) by averaging the neighboring cells. The mass, momentum and energy of the averaged fields are computed after downsampling as shown in Figure 25. In contrast, the conservative method operates on the conserved quantities such as the momentum (ρuV）和能量（) and energy (ρEV），确保下采样分辨率的守恒，并随后提取感兴趣的原始变量，例如速度。保守下采样得到的守恒量的绝对误差和相对误差都是机器精度的量级。另一方面，通过对细网格上的 y 速度进行平均而获得的 y 动量存在显着差异 - 相对误差范围为), ensuring conservation in the downsampled resolution and subsequently extracting the primitive variables of interest such as velocity. The absolute and relative errors in the conserved quantities obtained from conservative downsampling are in the order of machine precision. On the other hand, significant discrepancies are observed in the y-momentum, obtained from averaging the y-velocities on the fine grid - with relative errors ranging fromO (10−_2 ) - _O(10)，强调了保守下采样方法的必要性。尽管初始时间步长期间 y 动量的相对误差约为(10), highlighting the need for a conservative downsampling approach. Although the relative error in the y-momentum during the initial timesteps is in the order ofO (104），这主要是因为原始网格上的对应值几乎为零，除以如此小的数字会导致较大的相对误差。), this is primarily because the corresponding values on the original grid are nearly zero and division by such small numbers results in large relative errors.

为了深入了解这两种下采样方法的影响，我们在图 26 中可视化了原始分辨率 - (a) 和下采样分辨率 - (b) 和 (c) 上的 y 动量场。当将下采样场与原始网格进行比较时，通过平均 y 速度获得的值在界面处显示出显着的误差，而使用保守下采样方法时不存在这些误差。To gain insights into the impact of both downsampling methods, we visualize in Figure 26 the y-momentum field on the original resolution - (a) and the downsampled resolution - (b) and (c). When comparing the downsampled field to the original grid, the values obtained by averaging the y-velocities shows significant errors at the interface, whereas these errors are not present when using the conservative downsampling method.

B 基准测试详细信息B Benchmarking Details

B.1 基线模型B.1 Baseline Models

我们提供了有关基准测试实验中使用的基线模型的更多详细信息。我们的评估涵盖了通过随机初始化从头开始训练的各种最先进的架构，包括卷积模型（CNeXt、CNO）、谱模型（FFNO）和基于变压器的架构（ScOT）。此外，我们还微调了两个预训练模型：Poseidon-B 和 DPOT-M。为基线选择的超参数的详细信息如下：We present additional details on the baseline models used in our benchmarking experiments. Our evaluation covers a diverse set of state-of-the-art architectures trained from scratch with random initialization, including convolutional models (CNeXt, CNO), spectral models (FFNO), and transformer-based architectures (ScOT). In addition, we fine-tune two pretrained models: Poseidon-B and DPOT-M. Details on the chosen hyperparameters for the baselines are as follows:

  1. 卷积神经算子（CNO）：Convolutional Neural Operator (CNO):CNO 与 U-Net 非常相似，通过一系列编码器层处理输入函数，这些编码器层逐渐降低空间分辨率，同时增加通道深度。然后，该编码表示被传递到解码器层，解码器层通过恢复空间分辨率和减少通道数量来反转该过程。在相应的空间尺度（或谱带），编码器和解码器特征通过 ResNet 式的跳跃连接进行连接。A CNO, much like a U-Net, processes an input function through a sequence of encoder layers that progressively decrease spatial resolution while increasing channel depth. This encoded representation is then passed to decoder layers, which reverse the process by restoring spatial resolution and reducing the number of channels. At corresponding spatial scales (or spectral bands), encoder and decoder features are connected via ResNet-style skip connections.

  2. 该架构的核心思想是其上采样和下采样方案，其中应用低通滤波以避免在重采样期间引入人工高频分量。这种方法符合香农采样定理，确保离散表示与底层连续函数保持一致。The central idea of the architecture is its upsampling and downsampling scheme, where low-pass filtering is applied to avoid introducing artificial high-frequency components during resampling. This approach aligns with the Shannon sampling theorem, ensuring that discrete representations remain consistent with the underlying continuous functions.

  1. ConvNeXt（CNeXt）：ConvNeXt (CNeXt):ConvNeXt 是一种现代卷积神经网络架构，旨在弥补传统 CNN 和视觉 Transformer 之间的性能差距。它建立在经典 ResNet 设计的基础上，但融合了一些受 Transformer 启发的更新，例如大内核深度卷积、层归一化、GELU 激活和基于补丁的茎。 ConvNeXt 采用具有多个阶段的分层模型，逐渐降低空间分辨率，同时增加通道容量，可作为强大而高效的特征提取器，适用于各种视觉任务。尽管保持完全卷积，其设计实现了与基于变压器的模型相当的性能。 3D 数据集在 10M 变体上进行训练，2D 数据集在 50M 变体上进行训练。A ConvNeXt is a modern convolutional neural network architecture introduced to bridge the performance gap between traditional CNNs and vision transformers. It builds on the classical ResNet design but incorporates several transformer-inspired updates, such as large-kernel depthwise convolutions, Layer Normalization, GELU activations, and a patch-based stem. Organized as a hierarchical model with multiple stages that progressively reduce spatial resolution while increasing channel capacity, ConvNeXt serves as a powerful and efficient feature extractor for a wide range of vision tasks. Despite remaining fully convolutional, its design achieves performance comparable to transformer-based models. The 3D datasets are trained on the 10M variant and the 2D datasets are trained on the 50M variant.

  1. 因式分解傅里叶神经算子（FFNO）：Factorized Fourrier Neural Operator(FFNO):与 FNO 一样，它在傅立叶域中运行以捕获全局交互，但 FFNO 不是学习完整的多维谱核，而是将算子分解为沿每个空间维度应用的一维傅立叶变换序列。这种可分离的公式显着减少了参数数量和计算成本，同时保持了强大的Like FNO, it operates in the Fourier domain to capture global interactions, but instead of learning a full multi-dimensional spectral kernel, FFNO factorizes the operator into a sequence of one-dimensional Fourier transformations applied along each spatial dimension. This separable formulation significantly reduces the number of parameters and computational cost while maintaining strong

36

表达力。与 ConvNeXt 类似，3D 数据集使用 10M 变量进行训练，而 2D 数据集使用 50M 变量进行训练。expressive power. Similar to ConvNeXt, the 3D datasets are trained using the 10M variant, while the 2D datasets are trained using the 50M variant.

MLP 中的激活Activation in MLP ReLU ReLU

  1. 可扩展算子变压器 (ScOT) 和 Poseidon：Scalable Operator Transformer (ScOT) and Poseidon:ScOT 模型建立在 Poseidon 框架之上（Herde et al., 2024），其中 Poseidon 为学习可泛化解算子提供了基础模型框架，ScOT 作为其核心架构支柱。 ScOT 的核心采用了受视觉变压器启发的分层变压器设计，使用基于窗口的机制，其中输入域被划分为非重叠补丁的统一网格，并增加了对非方形输入的支持。每个补丁通过共享空间加权方案进行平均，然后线性投影到由潜在通道数量定义的潜在嵌入空间中，产生分段恒定的潜在表示，减少全局注意力的成本，同时保留基本的局部结构。然后，这种嵌入式表示通过多个阶段的 SwinV2 Transformer 块（Liu 等人，2021）进行处理，这些块以类似 UNet 的层次结构排列，其中特征图逐渐下采样，然后上采样，每个阶段的块数量由“深度”超参数控制。在每个阶段中，注意力都是使用窗口多头自注意力（MHSA）计算的，限制与局部区域的交互以提高效率，而层之间移动的窗口则可以实现跨窗口通信并捕获全局上下文。编码器采用补丁合并来降低空间分辨率并增加学习全局模式的特征维度，而解码器通过补丁扩展恢复分辨率，并通过 ConvNeXt 块实现跳跃连接（Liu 等人，2022）（由“跳跃连接”超参数控制）链接相应的编码器和解码器阶段以保留细粒度信息。The ScOT model is built upon the Poseidon framework (Herde et al., 2024), where Poseidon provides the foundationmodel framework for learning generalizable solution operators, and ScOT serves as its core architectural backbone. At its core, ScOT employs a hierarchical transformer design inspired by vision transformers, using a window-based mechanism in which the input domain is divided into a uniform grid of non-overlapping patches, with added support for non-square inputs. Each patch is averaged via a shared spatial weighting scheme and then linearly projected into a latent embedding space defined by the number of latent channels, yielding a piecewise-constant latent representation that reduces the cost of global attention while retaining essential local structure. This embedded representation is then processed through multiple stages of SwinV2 Transformer blocks (Liu et al., 2021) arranged in a UNet-like hierarchy, where feature maps are progressively downsampled and later upsampled, with the number of blocks per stage governed by the “depths” hyperparameter. Within each stage, attention is computed using windowed multi-head self-attention (MHSA), restricting interactions to local regions for efficiency, while shifted windows between layers enable cross-window communication and capture global context. The encoder incorporates patch merging to decrease spatial resolution and increase feature dimensionality for learning global patterns, whereas the decoder restores resolution via patch expansion, with skip connections implemented through ConvNeXt blocks (Liu et al., 2022)—controlled by the “skip-connections” hyperparameter—linking corresponding encoder and decoder stages to preserve fine-grained information.

  1. 去噪预训练算子变压器 (DPOT)：Denoising Pre-trained Operator Transformer (DPOT):DPOT 是一种大规模神经算子架构，旨在学习偏微分方程的解映射。它围绕傅里叶变换器主干构建，在频域中执行注意力以有效捕获全局空间依赖性，使模型能够逼近函数空间上的积分算子。该架构将时间聚合层与多头傅里叶注意层结合起来，对来自多个时间步长的信息进行编码，该层在傅里叶空间中应用可学习的变换来对复杂的多尺度动态进行建模。此外，DPOT 与自回归去噪预训练策略相结合，该模型可以根据噪声损坏的输入来预测未来状态，以提高鲁棒性和泛化性。这种设计允许 DPOT 扩展到非常大的模型和DPOT is a large-scale neural operator architecture designed to learn solution mappings of PDEs. It is built around a Fourier transformer backbone, where attention is performed in the frequency domain to efficiently capture global spatial dependencies, enabling the model to approximate integral operators over function spaces. The architecture combines temporal aggregation layers to encode information from multiple time steps with multi-head Fourier attention layers, which apply learnable transformations in Fourier space to model complex multi-scale dynamics. Furthermore, DPOT is paired with an auto-regressive denoising pre-training strategy, where the model predicts future states from noise-corrupted inputs to improve robustness and generalization. This design allows DPOT to scale to very large models and

37

数据集，作为偏微分方程的基础模型，可以在不同的下游任务中进行微调。datasets, functioning as a foundation model for PDEs that can be fine-tuned across diverse downstream tasks.

B.2 训练协议B.2 Training Protocol

正如正文中提到的，我们对随机权重初始化的模型采用多对一的训练策略，其中 4 个历史快照作为输入传递给模型，预测下一个快照并将其与目标进行比较。我们使用此设置对上一节中提到的基线进行基准测试，但基准测试存储库也能够处理多对多场景。对于预训练模型，我们遵循各自作品中指定的原始输入输出配置。As mentioned in the main text, we employ a many-to-one training strategy for the models intialized with random weights, where 4 historic snapshots are passed to the model as input and the next snapshot is predicted and compared with the target. We use this setup for benchmarking the baselines mentioned in the previous section, but the benchmarking repository is capabale of handling manyto-many scenarios as well. For the pre-trained models, we adhere to the original input–output configurations specified in their respective works.

我们的基准测试存储库的一个关键核心功能是能够通过自定义归一化层将调节参数集成到所有基线架构中（图 27），其中中间特征统计数据通过从调节向量导出的学习尺度和移位项进行调制。考虑自定义规范的输入，A key central feature of our benchmarking repository is the ability to integrate conditioning parameters into all baseline architectures via custom normalization layers (Figure 27) where intermediate feature statistics are modulated through learned scale and shift terms derived from the conditioning vector. Consider an input to the custom norm,x ∈ R长×宽×长B×S×L, 其中, whereB表示批量大小，denotes the batch size,S表示空间分辨率，并且represent the spatial resolution, andL是潜在维度。自定义范数类似于标准 LayerNorm (Ba et al., 2016)，修改为仿射参数 (is the latent dimension. The custom norm is similar to a standard LayerNorm (Ba et al., 2016), with the modification that the affine parameters (γθ和andβθ) 是条件参数向量的函数) are made functions of the conditioning parameter vectorc（Perez 等人，2017），其中(Perez et al., 2017), wherec εc ∈ R宽×宽B×F, 和, andF是调节特征的数量。is the number of conditioning features.

图 27：AdaNorm 调节模块图示，适用于所有基线。Figure 27: Illustration of the AdaNorm conditioning module, applicable to all baselines.F表示调节参数的数量，并且denotes the number of conditioning parameters, andL潜在维度。the latent dimension.

38

在我们的基准测试存储库中，我们实现了几种其他调节方法，但在这项工作中，我们重点关注自适应标准化（AdaNorm），它是调节特征向量的简单且轻量级的映射In our benchmarking repository, we have several other conditioning methods implemented, but in this work we focus on Adaptive Normalization (AdaNorm), which is a simple and lightweight mapping of the conditioning feature vectorc，通过 MLP 得到所需的潜在维度, through an MLP to the desired latent dimensionL .

B.3 评估协议B.3 Evaluation Protocol

模型性能在保留的测试集上进行评估。在推理过程中，每个模型都以自回归方式应用，从初始条件开始，一直延伸到测试轨迹的整个长度。Model performance is evaluated on a held-out test set. During inference, each model is applied autoregressively, starting from the initial condition and rolling out for the full length of a test trajectory.

推理产生形状的预测和目标张量 (Inference produces prediction and target tensors of shape (N、R、T、C、SN, R, T, C, S），其中), whereN是测试轨迹的数量，is the number of test trajectories,R推出步骤的数量，以及the number of rollout steps, andT每次推出的预测时间步数。每个度量仅在空间维度上减少，产生形状的误差张量the number of predicted timesteps per rollout. Each metric is reduced over the spatial dimensions only, yielding an error tensor of shapeE εE ∈ RN×(RT)×C。这保留了对轨迹、预测时间和通道的依赖性。. This preserves the dependence on trajectory, prediction time, and channel.

生成的张量可以进行多个级别的评估。首先，轨迹维度的平均值和标准差计算如下The resulting tensor enables evaluation at multiple levels. First, the mean and standard deviation over the trajectory dimension are computed asµ, σ εµ, σ ∈ R(RT)×C，它描述了误差的时间演变和测试轨迹的变异性，为每个通道单独报告。, which describe the temporal evolution of the error and the variability across test trajectories, reported separately for each channel.

每个指标的标量总体损失A scalar overall loss for each metricµ测试数据集所有轨迹的总体是通过聚合剩余维度获得的overall across all trajectories of the test dataset is obtained by aggregating over the remaining dimensions ofµ，即根据该度量所使用的公式，在预测时间和通道上。表 22 到表 51 显示了本研究中针对不同数据集的所有指标的标量总体损失。, i.e. over prediction time and channels, in accordance with the formulation used by that metric. Tables 22 through 51 showcase this scalar overall loss for all the metrics in this work for different datasets.

B.4 指标B.4 Metrics

正如正文中提到的，我们从度量角度执行两组消融。在第一组实验中，我们仅使用 MSE 损失来训练基线，在第二组实验中，我们使用复合损失来训练基线，复合损失是多个指标的组合：MSE、H1、SSIM 和 IRMSE。表 12 中指定了这些指标的初始权重。在推理过程中，我们根据更广泛的指标集评估训练基线的性能，如表 13 中所述。As mentioned in the main text, we perform two sets of ablations from a metric perspective. In the first set of experiments, we perform training of the baselines with only MSE loss and in the second set of experiments, we train the baselines with a composite loss which is a combination of several metrics: MSE, H1, SSIM and IRMSE. The initial weights for these metrics is specified in Table 12. During inference, we evaluate the performance of the trained baselines on a broader set of metrics, as mentioned in Table 13.

IRMSE 和 SRMSE 都可选地包括高斯平滑，如原始 GRMSE 公式 Cooper-Baldock 等人所提议的那样。 （2024）。具有标准偏差的高斯滤波器Both IRMSE and SRMSE optionally include Gaussian smoothing, as proposed in the original GRMSE formulation Cooper-Baldock et al. (2024). A Gaussian filter with standard deviationσ在计算损失之前应用于加权掩模。这是作为可分离卷积实现的，其中一维高斯核沿着每个空间轴顺序应用。平滑减少了权重场中的高频噪声，并在训练过程中产生更稳定的梯度。is applied to the weighting masks before computing the loss. This is implemented as separable convolution, where a one-dimensional Gaussian kernel is applied sequentially along each spatial axis. The smoothing reduces high-frequency noise in the weighting fields and leads to more stable gradients during training.

B.4.3 频谱度量B.4.3 Spectral metrics

在这项工作中，谱分解是使用多级 DWT 实现的。关键思想是根据不同空间尺度上的空间局部频率内容来表示一个场。在每个分解级别In this work, spectral decomposition is implemented using the multi-level DWT. The key idea is to represent a field in terms of its spatially localized frequency content across different spatial scales. At each decomposition leveli，输入场分为低频分量和高频分量，, the input field is split into a low-frequency component and a high-frequency component,

^ 类似地进行预测ˆ and analogously for the predictiony ,

低频分量The low-frequency component低频LFi包含场的粗略、缓慢变化的结构，而高频分量contains the coarse, slowly varying structure of the field, while the high-frequency component高频HFi捕捉更精细的细节。然后递归地应用变换captures finer-scale detail. The transform is then applied recursively

43

仅针对低频分量。重复此过程only to the low-frequency component. Repeating this procedure overI水平产生一个最终的粗略近似值levels yields one final coarse approximation线性FILFI连同细节系数的层次结构，together with a hierarchy of detail coefficients,

哪里whereHF1 对应于最精细的细节，更高的级别代表逐渐粗糙的空间尺度（图 31）。1 corresponds to the finest details and higher levels represent progressively coarser spatial scales (Figure 31).

图 31：DWT 的概念图，递归分解输入Figure 31: Conceptual illustration of the DWT, recursively decomposing an inputy转化为频带贡献。into frequencyband contributions.

对于多维字段，变换沿每个空间维度独立应用。这会产生方向细节子带（例如，二维中的水平、垂直和对角线特征）。这些方向分量共同构成了一组高频系数For multidimensional fields, the transform is applied independently along each spatial dimension. This produces directional detail subbands (e.g., horizontal, vertical, and diagonal features in 2D). Collectively, these directional components form the set of high-frequency coefficients高频HFi处于水平at leveli。在这项工作中，遵循 Prantl 等人在相关研究中的使用，使用了正交 Daubechies 小波。 （2022）。. In this work, orthogonal Daubechies wavelets are used, following their usage in related studies Prantl et al. (2022).

对数缩放使得损耗对精细尺度结构（即薄界面和小尺度振荡）的差异更加敏感。当应用于具有多个时间步长的轨迹时，损失可选地包括时间维度上的单独的一维小波变换，在每个空间位置独立应用。在这种情况下，损失被构造为空间和时间贡献的总和，其中The logarithmic scaling makes the more loss sensitive to discrepancies in fine-scale structure (i.e., thin interfaces and small-scale oscillations). When applied to trajectories with multiple timesteps, the loss optionally includes a separate 1D wavelet transform over the time dimension, applied independently at each spatial location. 这里Herey ˆ¯和andy¯ 表示预测和标签的平均值。相应的损失定义为：¯ denote the means of the predictions and labels. The corresponding loss is defined as:

PCC 在输入的仿射变换下是不变的，这使得它对场之间的均匀位移或全局缩放差异不敏感。最小化 1PCC is invariant under affine transformations of the inputs, making it insensitive to uniform shifts or global scaling differences between fields. Minimizing 1− r因此鼓励预测重现目标的整体协方差结构，促进特征的空间对齐。therefore encourages the prediction to reproduce the overall covariance structure of the target, promoting spatial alignment of features.

该指标衡量保守量总量的差异，与域内误差的空间分布方式无关（图 34）。表 19 总结了实施的数量。This metric measures discrepancies in the total amount of the conserved quantity, independent of how errors are distributed spatially within the domain (Figure 34). The implemented quantities are summarized in Table 19.

在实现中，在计算度量之前，所有预测字段和目标字段首先被转换回物理单位。为了保持结果值的良好缩放，守恒误差通过相应的目标量进行归一化，从而产生 nRMSE 式归一化，从而产生阶数的损失值In the implementation, all predicted and target fields are first converted back to physical units before computing the metric. To keep the resulting values well-scaled, the conservation error is normalized by the corresponding target quantity, resulting in an nRMSE-style normalization that yields loss values of orderO (1).

图 34：iqRMSE 指标。Figure 34: iqRMSE metric.

B.5 自适应损耗平衡框架B.5 Adaptive loss balancing framework

给定一组Given a set ofM损耗成分：loss components:{李}{Li}M i =1，标量训练目标写为, the scalar training objective is written as

哪里whereλi表示分配给损失分量的权重denotes the weight assigned to loss componenti .

图 35 说明了广义损失平衡框架。标量化训练损失A generalized loss-balancing framework is illustrated in Figure 35. The scalarized training lossJ ( θ) 通过公式 53 计算，并且对构成的损耗分量进行单独采样和存储。对于分量梯度也可以选择执行相同的操作，从而仅存储跨参数的聚合统计数据（此处由) is computed via Equation 53, and the constituent loss components are individually sampled and stored. The same is optionally performed for component gradients, whereby only aggregated statistics across parameters are stored (indicated here bygj）而不是全梯度张量，为了) as opposed to full gradient tensors, in order to

47

减少内存开销。这些历史记录构成了reduce memory overhead. These histories form the input to the损失加权策略loss weighting strategy。采样频率和更新频率可独立配置。默认情况下，损失值或梯度在每个训练步骤中采样，而损失权重每个时期更新一次。这使得可以在响应能力和计算开销之间进行权衡。在这项工作中，我们采用两种损失平衡策略：如算法 1 所示的 SoftAdapt 和如算法 2 所示的梯度归一化 (GradNorm)。. The sampling frequency and update frequency are configurable independently. By default, loss values or gradients are sampled at every training step, while the loss weights are updated once per epoch. This makes it possible to trade off responsiveness against computational overhead. In this work, we employ two loss balancing strategies: SoftAdapt as shown in algorithm 1 and the Gradient Normalization (GradNorm) as shown in algorithm 2.

图 35：损失平衡框架的概念图。Figure 35: Conceptual illustration of the loss balancing framework.

B.5.1 软适应B.5.1 SoftAdapt

SoftAdapt Heydari 等人。 （2019）根据损失分量的近期变化率调整损失分量的权重。关键思想是优先考虑损失减少较慢（或增加）的目标，这表明模型目前正在努力优化它们。为了估计这种行为，SoftAdapt 使用过去值的简短历史记录来近似每个损失分量的最近斜率。SoftAdapt Heydari et al. (2019) adjusts the weights of loss components according to their recent rate of change. The key idea is to prioritize objectives whose loss has decreased more slowly (or increased), indicating that the model is currently struggling to optimize them. To estimate this behavior, SoftAdapt approximates the recent slope of each loss component using a short history of past values.

让LetL( it)表示损耗分量的值denote the value of loss componenti在训练步骤at training stept。最近的变化率近似为. The recent rate of change is approximated ass( it) = L( it) −L( i_t−_1)。这些斜率通过 softmax 变换转换为损失分量权重. These slopes are converted into loss component weights through a softmax transformation

哪里whereβ是一个可调温度参数，用于控制加权对改善损失的效果的强调程度。对于is a tunable temperature parameter that controls how strongly the weighting emphasizes poorly improving losses. Forβ >0，正斜率较大的损失获得较高的权重，而0, losses with larger positive slopes receive higher weights, whileβ <0 有利于损失减少最快。环境0 favors losses that are decreasing fastest. Settingβ= 0 导致均匀加权。表 20 列出了为本次工作选择的超参数值。= 0 results in uniform weighting. The hyperparameter value chosen for this work is presented in Table 20.

B.5.2 梯度归一化（GradNorm）B.5.2 Gradient Normalization (GradNorm)

GradNorm（Chen et al., 2017）更新损失分量权重，使得每个加权损失分量的梯度范数与由该分量的相对训练速度确定的目标相匹配。在实践中，该方法应用于共享参数子集GradNorm (Chen et al., 2017) updates the loss component weights such that the gradient norm of each weighted loss component matches a target determined by the component’s relative training speed. In practice, the method is applied to a shared parameter subsetθ sh ⊂ θ，通常是最后一个共享层。对于任务, typically the last shared layer. For taski，梯度范数定义为, the gradient norm is defined asG( θi) = ∥∇θ ( λiLi ) ∥2. 目标梯度范数2. B.6 计算资源B.6 Computational Resources

高保真 2D 数据集和 3D-SDBA (SSOOSS) 数据集的每个轨迹都是使用具有 112 个 CPU 核心的单个 Intel-Xeon(R) Platinum 8480+ 节点在 HPC 集群上生成的，而单个 3D-SABW (SSOOSS) 轨迹是在具有 24 个 CPU 核心的 Intel Skylake Xeon Platinum 8174 的单个节点上生成的。Each trajectory of the high fidelity 2D-datasets and the 3D-SDBA (SSOOSS) datasets was generated on an HPC-cluster using a single Intel-Xeon(R) Platinum 8480+ node with 112 CPU-cores, while a single 3D-SABW (SSOOSS) trajectory was generated on a single node of Intel Skylake Xeon Platinum 8174 with 24 CPU-cores.

对于训练代理模型，我们在 4 个 NVIDIA-A100 GPU（每个具有 40GB VRAM）上采用数据分布式并行策略作为 2D 基线，而 3D 基线则在 4 个 NVIDIA-H100 GPU（每个具有 90GB VRAM）上进行训练。For training surrogate models, we employ a Data Distributed Parallel strategy on 4 NVIDIA-A100 GPUs each with 40GB of VRAM for the 2D baselines, while the 3D baselines were trained on 4 NVIDIA-H100 GPUs, each with 90GB VRAM.

49

C 详细结果C Detailed results

C.1 指标性能比较C.1 Metric performance comparison

图 36 显示了使用 FFNO 和两种不同训练策略的 2D-SDBA 轨迹最后一个时间步的预测质量差异，以及与地面实况的比较。可以看出，具有更好 SSIM 值的预测与真实情况具有更高的结构一致性，即使 RMSE 表明两者的预测同样良好。Figure 36 shows the difference in the prediction quality for the last timestep of a 2D-SDBA trajectory using FFNO with two different training strategies and also comparison with the ground truth. It can be observed that the predictions having better SSIM values have more structural coherence with the ground truth even though RMSE indicates equally good predictions for both.

图 36：(a) t=70 秒时随机选择的 2D-SDBA 数据集的参考密度快照。 (b) 使用复合损失训练相应的 FFNO (50M) 预测，并进一步使用 GradNorm 自适应损失加权策略。 RMSE = 0.46，SSIM = 0.28。(c) 仅使用 MSE 损失训练相应的 FFNO 预测。 RMSE = 0.46，SSIM = 0.32Figure 36: (a) Reference density snapshot for a randomly chosen 2D-SDBA dataset at t=70 s. RMSE = 0.46 and SSIM = 0.28.(c) Corresponding FFNO prediction trained with only MSE loss. RMSE = 0.46 and SSIM = 0.32

由于正文中的结果表明使用具有自适应损失加权策略的复合损失具有明显的优势，因此一个自然的问题是这些方法产生的额外计算成本。我们从两个角度研究这种权衡：峰值 GPU 内存需求（如图 37 所示）和完成一个 epoch 所需的训练时间（如图 38 所示）。由于 GradNorm 需要梯度信息来适应损失权重，因此预计会引入额外的内存和计算开销。相比之下，SoftAdapt 不需要梯度存储，因此在内存使用和每个周期的训练时间方面引入的开销可以忽略不计。As the results in the main text indicate clear advantages of using composite losses with adaptive loss-weighting strategies, a natural question concerns the additional computational cost incurred by these methods. We examine this trade-off from two perspectives: the peak GPU memory requirement, shown in Figure 37, and the training time required to complete one epoch, shown in Figure 38. Since GradNorm requires gradient information to adapt the loss weights, it is expected to introduce additional memory and computational overhead. In contrast, SoftAdapt does not require gradient storage and therefore introduces negligible overhead in both memory usage and training time per epoch.

C.2 推论度量随推出步骤的演变C.2 Inference metric evolution over rollout steps

本节介绍了测试数据集轨迹的推理指标在部署步骤中的演变。在每个推出步骤中，对测试数据集中所有轨迹的指标进行平均，相应的平均值和标准差如下图所示。这些图按数据集组织，每个图对应一个特定的数据集，并包含在该数据集上评估的所有基线和训练策略的结果。这些指标分为逐点指标、光谱指标、聚焦特征指标、结构感知指标和物理信息指标。 Poseidon-M 在本节中显示的所有图中都有不同的起点，因为第一个预测时间步长取决于基线所需的输入时间步长数量以及这些时间步长之间的步幅。This section presents the evolution of the inference metrics across rollout steps for the test dataset trajectories. The metrics are averaged across all trajectories in the test dataset at each rollout step and the corresponding mean and standard deviation are presented in the following figures. The figures are organized by dataset, with each figure corresponding to a specific dataset and containing the results for all baselines and training strategies evaluated on that dataset. The metrics are categorized into pointwise, spectral, feature-focused, structure-aware, and physics-informed. Poseidon-M has a different starting point in all the plots shown in this section, since the first prediction timestep depends on the number of input timesteps required by the baseline and the stride between those timesteps.

C.3 总体推理指标C.3 Overall inference metrics

下表报告了长期部署推理指标。对于测试数据集中的每个轨迹，自回归应用经过训练的基线模型，从初始条件开始一直持续到轨迹结束。 C.2 节介绍了测试数据集每次部署的所有轨迹的平均指标演变。本节中介绍的最终指标是通过对所有轨迹和推出步骤的指标值进行平均而获得的。这些表按数据集组织，每个表对应一个特定数据集，并包含在该数据集上评估的所有基线和训练策略的结果。这些指标分为逐点指标、光谱指标、聚焦特征指标、结构感知指标和物理信息指标。The following tables report the long-term rollout inference metrics. For each trajectory in the test dataset, the trained baseline model is applied autoregressively, starting from the initial condition and continuing until the end of the trajectory. The evolution of the metrics, averaged across all the trajectories, for each rollout for the test dataset is presented in section C.2. The final metric presented in this section is obtained by averaging the metric values across all trajectories and rollout steps. The tables are organized by dataset, with each table corresponding to a specific dataset and containing the results for all baselines and training strategies evaluated on that dataset. The metrics are categorized into pointwise, spectral, feature-focused, structure-aware, and physics-informed.

表 22：2D-SABW (OOOO) 测试数据集上基线的逐点评估，报告所有字段和所有推出步骤的逐点字段值（MAE、RMSE、VRMSE）和字段导数（H1、H2）的平均误差。值越低越好。较深的阴影表示一组训练损失策略中每个基线的值较低。粗体值表示给定指标的所有基线中最低的值，下划线值表示第二低的值。Table 22: Pointwise evaluation of baselines on the 2D-SABW (OOOO) test dataset, reporting errors in pointwise field values (MAE, RMSE, VRMSE) and the field derivatives (H1, H2) averaged across all fields and all rollout steps. Lower values are better. Darker shade indicates lower value for each baseline within a group of training loss strategies. Bold values denote the lowest among all baselines for a given metric and the underlined values denote the second-lowest values.

型号Model 训练损失Training loss H1 H2 MAE 均方根误差RMSE 均方根误差VRMSE
