NeurIPS 论文清单NeurIPS Paper Checklist

1. 索赔Claims

问题：摘要和引言中提出的主要主张是否准确反映了论文的贡献和范围？Question: Do the main claims made in the abstract and introduction accurately reflect the paper’s contributions and scope?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：摘要中提出的主张包含在正文的第 3 节和第 5 节中。更多信息详见附录 C 部分。Justification: The claims made in the abstract are covered in sections 3 and 5 of the main text. Further information are detailed in the appendix section C.

  1. 局限性Limitations

问题：本文是否讨论了作者所做工作的局限性？答案：[是]Question: Does the paper discuss the limitations of the work performed by the authors? Answer: [Yes]

理由：我们在正文第 5.2 节中明确提供了限制。Justification: We have provided the limitations clearly in the section 5.2 of the main text.

  1. 理论假设和证明Theory assumptions and proofs

问题：对于每个理论结果，论文是否提供了全套假设和完整（且正确）的证明？Question: For each theoretical result, does the paper provide the full set of assumptions and a complete (and correct) proof?

答案：[不适用]Answer: [N/A]

理由：本文不包括理论结果，而是侧重于冲击引起的多相流替代模型的经验基准测试。Justification: The paper does not include theoretical results, but rather focuses on the empirical benchmarking of surrogate models for shock-induced multiphase flows.

4. 实验结果重现性Experimental result reproducibility

问：论文是否充分披露了重现论文主要实验结果所需的所有信息，并达到影响论文主要主张和/或结论的程度（无论是否提供代码和数据）？Question: Does the paper fully disclose all the information needed to reproduce the main experimental results of the paper to the extent that it affects the main claims and/or conclusions of the paper (regardless of whether the code and data are provided or not)?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：为了生成数据集，我们在metadata.json 文件中提供了与物理和求解器相关的所有详细信息。为了重现基准，我们在 B.1 节中提供模型超参数，在 B.2 节中提供训练超参数，在 B.4 节中提供与指标和损失加权策略相关的超参数。此外，B.6 节中规定了进行培训的硬件规格。Justification: For generating the datasets, we provide all the details related to the physics and the solver in the metadata.json file. For reproducing the benchmarks we provide the model hyperparameters in section B.1, training hyperparameters in section B.2 and hyperparameters related to metrics and loss-weighting strategies in section B.4. Further the hardware specifications on which the training was carried out is specified in section B.6.

5. 开放数据和代码的访问Open access to data and code

问题：论文是否提供对数据和代码的开放访问，并提供足够的说明来忠实地再现主要实验结果，如补充材料中所述？Question: Does the paper provide open access to the data and code, with sufficient instructions to faithfully reproduce the main experimental results, as described in supplemental material?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：正文第一页的脚注中提供了开源（匿名）代码和数据集的链接。基准测试存储库的 README.md 包含设置虚拟环境、训练基线以及在推理过程中从训练模型生成指标所需的所有说明。Justification: The link to the open-sourced (anonymized) code and datasets is provided in the footnote of the first page of the main text. The README.md of the benchmarking repository contains all the necessary instructions to set up a virtual environment, train the baselines and generate metrics from the trained models during inference.

6. 实验设置/细节Experimental setting/details

问题：论文是否指定了理解结果所需的所有训练和测试细节（例如，数据分割、超参数、如何选择它们、优化器类型）？答案：[是]Question: Does the paper specify all the training and test details (e.g., data splits, hyperparameters, how they were chosen, type of optimizer) necessary to understand the results? Answer: [Yes]

理由：图 1 中提供了数据分割，B.1 节中提供了基线的超参数，B.2 节中提供了训练超参数，B.4 节中提供了与指标和损失加权策略相关的超参数Justification: The data-splits are provided in Figure 1, the hyperparameters for the baselines in section B.1, training hyperparameters in section B.2 and hyperparameters related to metrics and loss-weighting strategies in section B.4

  1. 实验统计显着性Experiment statistical significance

问题：论文是否报告了适当且正确定义的误差线或有关实验统计显着性的其他适当信息？Question: Does the paper report error bars suitably and correctly defined or other appropriate information about the statistical significance of the experiments?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：我们在 C.2 节中的 2D 和 3D 基线的度量演化图中提供了误差线。Justification: We provide error bars in our metric evolution plots for the 2D and 3D baselines in section C.2.

101

8. 实验计算资源Experiments compute resources

问题：对于每个实验，论文是否提供了重现实验所需的计算机资源（计算工作者类型、内存、执行时间）的足够信息？Question: For each experiment, does the paper provide sufficient information on the computer resources (type of compute workers, memory, time of execution) needed to reproduce the experiments?

答案：[是]，Answer:[Yes],

理由：生成数据集所需的挂钟时间在表 2 中指定。生成数据集和模型的硬件规格在 B.6 节中指定。 C.1 中列出了单个训练时期的峰值内存需求和挂钟时间。Justification: The wall clock time required to generate the datasets are specified in Table 2. The hardware specifications for generating the datasets and the models is specified in section B.6. The peak memory requirements and the wall clock time for a single training epoch is presented in C.1.

10. 更广泛的影响Broader impacts

答案：[不适用]Answer: [N/A]

理由：数据集和训练模型仅限于简化的基准配置，不能直接部署在现实世界的决策中。因此，预计不会产生直接的社会风险或负面影响，并且这项工作的更广泛的社会影响预计将主要保留在研究界内。Justification: The datasets and trained models are restricted to simplified benchmark configurations and are not directly deployable in real-world decision-making. As such, no immediate societal risks or negative impacts are anticipated, and the broader societal impact of this work is expected to remain primarily within the research community.

理由：本文提供的数据集和经过训练的代理旨在用于代理模型的科学基准测试和方法评估，而不是部署在安全关键或现实世界的操作系统中Justification: The datasets and the trained surrogates provided in this paper are intended for scientific benchmarking and methodological evaluation of surrogate models rather than deployment in safety-critical or real-world operational systems

13. 新资产New assets

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

The datasets are publicly hosted on Hugging Face, while the benchmarking repository is maintained on GitHub. All required licensing information is clearly specified in the README.md files. The baseline models used for benchmarking are adapted from existing open-source implementations, and appropriate credits and citations are provided within the benchmarking repository.
